<div class="awt-citation-component">
<h2 class="headline">Citation Machine — Write Smarter, Cite Accurately</h2>
<div class="headline-copy">Smart technology to create citations, provide grammar suggestions, and detect unintentional plagiarism.</div>
<ul class="action-button-set cm-default vertical">
<li class="action-button-shell awt equalize" id="paperchecker-cta-button">
<a href="https://www.citationmachine.net/grammar-and-plagiarism/">
<div class="action-button" id="paperchecker-cta-homepage"><span class="pc-cta-new-icon-v2">NEW</span>Upload your paper</div>
</a>
<div class="overlay-right">
<div class="curly-arrow-icon">
<img alt="Arrow left" src="//next-assets.citationmachine.net/assets/bibme/paperchecker-cta-experiment-1938/arrow-left-efabcacc07b0793f9eb43ac6cfd45ad7.png"/>
</div>
<div class="text-right" style="width: 418px;">
<span>Quickly add citations directly into your paper, and check for grammar, punctuation, and accidental plagiarism</span>
</div>
</div>
</li>
<li class="action-button-shell awt equalize" id="pc-get-back-to-citations">
<a href="/style">
<div class="action-button" id="">I only want to create citations</div>
</a>
</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div data-site-code="citationmachine" data-turbolinks-permanent="" id="pc-cta-react-root"><div class="feature-section"><h2 style="font-size: 25px;">Use Citation Machine Plus to get smart recommendations!</h2><p>Scan your paper for unintentional plagiarism and get advanced recommendations for sentence structure, writing style, grammar and more!</p><div class="hide-on-narrow"><h2>Recommendations include...</h2><div class="feature-select"><div class="row"><ul class="list-unstyled col-md-3 col-sm-3 col-xs-6"><li class="selected"><button class="btn md">Grammar</button></li><li><button class="btn md">Unintentional Plagiarism</button></li><li><button class="btn md">Sentence Structure</button></li><li><button class="btn md">Style</button></li><li><button class="btn md">Punctuation</button></li></ul><div class="feature-section col-md-9 col-sm-9 col-xs-6"><div class="paper-graphic grammar"><div class="feature-info feature-selected"><div class="text-graphic"></div><div class="feature-text" style="top: 30px;"><p>Use a first person plural verb with "we": are, were.</p><i class="fa fa-caret-right caret" style="top: 35px;"></i></div></div><div class="feature-info"><div class="text-graphic"></div><div class="feature-text" style="top: 85px;"><p><b>We found this sentence in 5 sources online.</b><br/> Do you want to cite this text?</p><i class="fa fa-caret-right caret" style="top: 5px;"></i></div></div><div class="feature-info"><div class="text-graphic"></div><div class="feature-text" style="top: 20px;"><p>The word "however" needs correct punctuation. If "however" separates two complete sentences, place a semicolon before it and a comma after: "; however,". If "however" is in the middle of a complete sentence, place a comma both before and after it: ",however,".</p><i class="fa fa-caret-right caret" style="top: 17px;"></i></div></div><div class="feature-info"><div class="text-graphic"></div><div class="feature-text" style="top: 75px;"><p>In formal writing, "And" is rarely--if ever--an acceptable way to begin a sentence.</p><i class="fa fa-caret-right caret" style="top: 15px;"></i></div></div><div class="feature-info"><div class="text-graphic"></div><div class="feature-text" style="top: 60px;"><p>The punctuation mark "," needs to be changed or deleted as "because" is rarely the last word of a sentence. </p><i class="fa fa-caret-right caret" style="top: 5px;"></i></div></div></div></div></div></div><a class="link awt-button"
